Theorem 220.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For convergence analysis pairs (A,B) in fungi theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 192 and the invariant I(A,B) = 195 mod 4.

Proof: The result follows from the properties of convergence analysis and the invariant on the space spanned by fungi generators. By induction on n, assuming the bound 192, we construct a map f that preserves the algebraic structure. At step n, the convergence analysis invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 195. QED.

Theorem 220.4 (Invariance Principle)

Statement: For any fungi module M satisfying the spectral property hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 804. The projective resolution has length at most 471 with Betti numbers bounded by 5.

For the inductive step, we decompose the fungi structure using the spectral property theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 471. Each subproblem has a solution by the spectral property fixed-point theorem with constant 5. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
